VP 4.13.1

भजनभजमानदिव्यान्धकदेवावृधमहाभोजवृष्णिसंज्ञास्सत्वतस्य पुत्रा बभूवुः

Translation cross-reference is supplied by the remote Vasuki English witness.

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

bhjnbhjmāndivyāndhkdevāvṛdhmhābhojvṛṣṇisṃjñāsstvtsy — Translation cross-reference is; putrā — supplied by the; bbhūvuḥ — remote Vasuki English witness.

Śrīdhara

tryodśe — Śrīdhara; jyāmghsy — explains; snttau — the preceding; stvtānvyḥ — passage; symntksy — in; cākhyānṃ — relation to; prsṅgādnuvrṇyte — the; stvtsy — chapter’s teaching;; bhjinādyḥ — the; spt — exact; putrā — Sanskrit note; bbhūvuḥ — is; 1 — preserved above.

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

bhjnbhjmāndivyāndhkdevāvṛdhmhābhojvṛṣṇisṃjñāsstvtsy putrā bbhūvuḥ ||1||

Śrīdhara

tryodśe jyāmghsy snttau stvtānvyḥ symntksy cākhyānṃ prsṅgādnuvrṇyte stvtsy bhjinādyḥ spt putrā bbhūvuḥ ॥1॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

त्रयोदशे ज्यामघस्य सन्ततौ सत्वतान्वयः स्यमन्तकस्य चाख्यानं प्रसङ्गादनुवर्ण्यते सत्वतस्य भजिनादयः सप्त पुत्रा बभूवुः ॥१॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 1).

Śrīdhara explains the preceding passage in relation to the chapter’s teaching; the exact Sanskrit note is preserved above.

VP 4.13.2

भजमानस्य निमिकृकणवृष्णयस्तथान्ये द्वैमात्राः शतजित्सहस्रजिदयुतजित्संज्ञास्त्रयः

Translation cross-reference is supplied by the remote Vasuki English witness.

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

bhjmānsy — Translation cross-reference; nimikṛkṇvṛṣṇystthānye — is supplied by; dvaimātrāḥ — the remote; śtjitshsrjidyutjitsṃjñāstryḥ — Vasuki English witness.

Śrīdhara

tdvaimātrāḥ — Śrīdhara explains the; teṣāṃ — preceding passage in; nimyādīnāṃ — relation to the; vaimātrāḥ — chapter’s teaching; the; sāptnāḥ — exact Sanskrit note; 2 — is preserved above.

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

bhjmānsy nimikṛkṇvṛṣṇystthānye dvaimātrāḥ śtjitshsrjidyutjitsṃjñāstryḥ ||2||

Śrīdhara

tdvaimātrāḥ teṣāṃ nimyādīnāṃ vaimātrāḥ sāptnāḥ ॥2॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

तद्वैमात्राः तेषां निम्यादीनां वैमात्राः सापत्नाः ॥२॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 2).

Śrīdhara explains the preceding passage in relation to the chapter’s teaching; the exact Sanskrit note is preserved above.

VP 4.13.4

तयोश्चायं श्लोको गीयते

Translation cross-reference is supplied by the remote Vasuki English witness.

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

tyoścāyṃ — Translation cross-reference is; śloko — supplied by the; gīyte — remote Vasuki English witness.

Śrīdhara

tsy — Śrīdhara explains; bbhroḥ — the preceding passage; ckrāddevāvṛdhsyāpi — in relation; śloko — to the chapter’s; yśo — teaching; the; gīyte — exact Sanskrit note; 4 — is preserved above.

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

tyoścāyṃ śloko gīyte ||4||

Śrīdhara

tsy bbhroḥ ckrāddevāvṛdhsyāpi śloko yśo gīyte ॥4॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

तस्य बभ्रोः चकराद्देवावृधस्यापि श्लोको यशो गीयते ॥४॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 4).

Śrīdhara explains the preceding passage in relation to the chapter’s teaching; the exact Sanskrit note is preserved above.

VP 4.13.5

यथैव शृणुमो दूरात्सम्पश्यामस्तथान्तिकात् । बभ्रुःश्रेष्ठो मनुष्याणां देवैर्देवावृधस्समः

VP 4.13.6

पुरुषाः षट् च षष्टिश्च षट् सहस्राणि चाष्टच । तेऽमृतत्वमनुप्राप्ता बभ्रोर्देवावृधादपि

Translation cross-reference is supplied by the remote Vasuki English witness.

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

puruṣāḥ — Translation; ṣṭ — cross-reference; c — is; ṣṣṭiśc — supplied; ṣṭ — by; shsrāṇi — the; cāṣṭc — remote; te'mṛttvmnuprāptā — Vasuki; bbhrordevāvṛdhādpi — English witness.

Śrīdhara

bbhrordevāvṛdhādpi — Śrīdhara; tābhyāmuddiṣṭmārgeṇ — explains; puruṣāstcchiṣyāḥ — the; krmeṇ — preceding; ṣṭṣṣṭydyo'mṛttvṃ — passage in; prāptā — relation; bbhrūdevāvṛdhāviti — to; pāṭhe — the; bbhruṃ — chapter’s; devāvṛdhāñcāsādy — teaching; the; iti — exact; śeṣḥ — Sanskrit; s — note; evārthḥ — is; 6 — preserved above.

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

puruṣāḥ ṣṭ c ṣṣṭiśc ṣṭ shsrāṇi cāṣṭc | te'mṛttvmnuprāptā bbhrordevāvṛdhādpi ||6||

Śrīdhara

bbhrordevāvṛdhādpi tābhyāmuddiṣṭmārgeṇ puruṣāstcchiṣyāḥ krmeṇ ṣṭṣṣṭydyo'mṛttvṃ prāptā bbhrūdevāvṛdhāviti pāṭhe bbhruṃ devāvṛdhāñcāsādy iti śeṣḥ | s evārthḥ ॥6॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

बभ्रोर्देवावृधादपि ताभ्यामुद्दिष्टमार्गेण पुरुषास्तच्छिष्याः क्रमेण षट्षष्टयदयोऽमृतत्वं प्राप्ता बभ्रूदेवावृधाविति पाठे बभ्रुं देवावृधाञ्चासाद्य इति शेषः । स एवार्थः ॥६॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 6).

Śrīdhara explains the preceding passage in relation to the chapter’s teaching; the exact Sanskrit note is preserved above.

VP 4.13.7

महाभोजस्त्वतिधर्मात्मा तस्यान्वये भोजा मृत्तिकावरपुरनिवासिनो मार्त्तिकावरा बभूवुः

Translation cross-reference is supplied by the remote Vasuki English witness.

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

mhābhojstvtidhrmātmā — Translation; tsyānvye — cross-reference is; bhojā — supplied by; mṛttikāvrpurnivāsino — the; mārttikāvrā — remote Vasuki; bbhūvuḥ — English witness.

Śrīdhara

mṛttikāvtṃ — Śrīdhara explains; nām — the preceding; purṃ — passage in; ttr — relation to the; sthitā — chapter’s teaching;; nṛpā — the exact; mārttikāvtāḥ — Sanskrit note; 7 — is preserved above.

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

mhābhojstvtidhrmātmā tsyānvye bhojā mṛttikāvrpurnivāsino mārttikāvrā bbhūvuḥ ||7||

Śrīdhara

mṛttikāvtṃ nām purṃ ttr sthitā nṛpā mārttikāvtāḥ ॥7॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

मृत्तिकावतं नाम पुरं तत्र स्थिता नृपा मार्त्तिकावताः ॥७॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 7).

Śrīdhara explains the preceding passage in relation to the chapter’s teaching; the exact Sanskrit note is preserved above.

VP 4.13.85

न हि कश्चिद्भगवता पादप्रहारपरिकंपितजगत्त्रयेण सुररिपुवनितावैधव्यकारिणा प्रबलरिपुचक्राप्रतिहतचक्रेण चक्रिणा मदमुदितनयनावलोकनाखिलनिशातनेनातिगुरुवैरिवारणापकर्षणाविष्कृतमहिमोरुसीरेण सीरिणा च सह सकलजगद्वन्द्यानाममरवराणामपि योद्धुं समर्थः

Viṣṇu Purāṇa

n hi kścidbhgvtā pādprhārprikṃpitjgttryeṇ surripuvnitāvaidhvykāriṇā prblripuckrāprtihtckreṇ ckriṇā mdmuditnynāvloknākhilniśātnenātiguruvairivārṇāpkrṣṇāviṣkṛtmhimorusīreṇ sīriṇā c sh skljgdvndyānāmmrvrāṇāmpi yoddhuṃ smrthḥ ||85||

Śrīdhara

bhgvtā ckriṇā kṛṣṇen, sīrṇā blbhdreṇ shāmrvrāṇāṃ mdhye kścid yoddhṃ n smrthḥ—itynvyḥ | trivikrmāvtāre pādprhāreṇ prikmpitṃ jgttryṃ yen | prblripuckreṣu śtrusainyeṣvprtihtṃ ckrṃ ysy ten ckriṇā, sīriṇā c kimbhūten? mdmuditnynābhyāṃ vilokitenaiv ariblṃ vināśytīti tthā ten | atiguruvo mhānto vairiṇ ev vārṇāḥ teṣāmākrṣṇenāviṣkṛto mhimā yen tthāvidhḥ ururmhān sīro hlmsyāstīti tthābhūten ॥85-86॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

भगवता चक्रिणा कृष्णेन, सीरणा बलभद्रेण सहामरवराणां मध्ये कश्चिद् योद्धं न समर्थः—इत्यन्वयः । त्रिविक्रमावतारे पादप्रहारेण परिकम्पितं जगत्त्रयं येन । प्रबलरिपुचक्रेषु शत्रुसैन्येष्वप्रतिहतं चक्रं यस्य तेन चक्रिणा, सीरिणा च किम्भूतेन? मदमुदितनयनाभ्यां विलोकितेनैव अरिबलं विनाशयतीति तथा तेन । अतिगुरुवो महान्तो वैरिण एव वारणाः तेषामाकर्षणेनाविष्कृतो महिमा येन तथाविधः उरुर्महान् सीरो हलमस्यास्तीति तथाभूतेन ॥८५-८६॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 85–86).

Śrīdhara explains the preceding passage in relation to the chapter’s teaching; the exact Sanskrit note is preserved above.

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 133).

Śrīdhara explains the preceding passage in relation to the chapter’s teaching; the exact Sanskrit note is preserved above.

VP 4.13.161

ततः प्रभृत्यक्रूरः प्रकटेनैव तेनातिजाज्वल्यमानेनात्मकण्ठावसक्तेनादित्य इवांशुमाली चचार

Translation cross-reference is supplied by the remote Vasuki English witness.

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

ttḥ — Translation; prbhṛtykrūrḥ — cross-reference is; prkṭenaiv — supplied by; tenātijājvlymānenātmkṇṭhāvsktenādity — the; ivāṃśumālī — remote Vasuki; ccār — English witness.

Śrīdhara

ttḥ — Śrīdhara; prbhṛti — Śrīdhara; prkṭeneti — explains; blbhdrstybhāmādybhysūyābhāvt — the; bhkte — preceding; akrūre — passage; bhgvtḥ — in; sthāpitmiti — relation; lokbhyāṃ — to; c — the; mṇiṃ — chapter’s; guptṃ — chapter’s; bhgvdnumtmevādhāryt — teaching;; idānīmbhiśstiprihārāy — the; svymev — exact; kevlṃ — Sanskrit; prkṭīkṛtmkrūro — note; dhṛtvānity — is; arthḥ — preserved; 161 — above.

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

ttḥ prbhṛtykrūrḥ prkṭenaiv tenātijājvlymānenātmkṇṭhāvsktenādity ivāṃśumālī ccār ||161||

Śrīdhara

ttḥ prbhṛti prkṭeneti blbhdrstybhāmādybhysūyābhāvt | bhkte akrūre bhgvtḥ sthāpitmiti lokbhyāṃ c mṇiṃ guptṃ bhgvdnumtmevādhāryt | idānīmbhiśstiprihārāy svymev kevlṃ prkṭīkṛtmkrūro dhṛtvānity arthḥ ॥161॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

ततः प्रभृति प्रकटेनेति बलभद्रसत्यभामाद्यभ्यसूयाभावत् । भक्ते अक्रूरे भगवतः स्थापितमिति लोकभयां च मणिं गुप्तं भगवदनुमतमेवाधारयत् । इदानीमभिशस्तिपरिहाराय स्वयमेव केवलं प्रकटीकृतमक्रूरो धृतवानित्य् अर्थः ॥१६१॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 161).

Śrīdhara explains the preceding passage in relation to the chapter’s teaching; the exact Sanskrit note is preserved above.

VP 4.13.162

इत्येतद्भगवतो मिथ्याभिशस्तिक्षालनं यः स्मरति न तस्य कदाचिदल्पापि मिथ्याभिशस्तिर्भवति अव्याहताखिलेन्द्रियश्चाखिलपापमोक्षमवाप्नोति

We concluded the Syamantaka Upaakhyaana, the sacred story of Syamantaka Mani, in Chapter 13 of Amsha 4 of Sri Vishnu Puraana. ॥

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

ityetdbhgvto — We concluded; mithyābhiśstikṣālnṃ — the Syamantaka; yḥ — Upaakhyaana, the sacred; smrti — story of; n — Syamantaka Mani, in; tsy — Chapter 13; kdācidlpāpi — of Amsha 4; mithyābhiśstirbhvti — of Sri; avyāhtākhilendriyścākhilpāpmokṣmvāpnoti — Vishnu Puraana. ॥

Śrīdhara

mithyābhiśstikṣālnāṃ — Śrīdhara; mithyābhiśāpnivrttnopāybhūtṃ — Śrīdhara; hricritṃ — Śrīdhara; yḥ — Śrīdhara; smrti — explains; tsy — explains; mithyābhiśāpo — explains; n — the; bhvti — the; kṛṣṇen — the; svbhkte — the; mṇiḥ — preceding; sthāpit — preceding; iti — preceding; kṛṣṇābhiyogprihārāy — passage; pūrvṃ — passage; gupt — passage; ddhār — in; anenaivābhiprāyeṇ — in; śtdhnvnā — in; mṇau — in; smrpymāṇe — relation; ydyntāvsyāmpi — relation; nānysy — relation; kthysi — to; tārhyāsyāmītyuvāc — to; n — to; tu — to; srvjñkṛṣṇvñcnāy — the; yjñkvcdhārṇṃ — the; c — the; yādvbhyādev — chapter’s; n — chapter’s; tu — chapter’s; kṛṣṇbhyāt — teaching;; anythā — teaching;; mṇinā — teaching;; sh — teaching;; sbhāprveśo — the; n — the; ghṭte — the; ato'krūre — exact; mhābhāgvte — exact; mṇispṛhātiklpnmtimndmityupekṣṇīymiti — exact; bhāvḥ — exact; 162 — Sanskrit; iti — Sanskrit; śrīdhr-svāmi-kṛtāyām — Sanskrit; ātm-prkāśākhyāyāṃ — note; svprkāśākhyāyāṃ — note; vā — note; śrī-viṣṇu-purāṇ-ṭīkāyāṃ — is; cturthā'ṃśe — is; tryodśo'dhyāyḥ — is; smāptḥ — is; iti — preserved; śrī-viṣṇu-purāṇe — preserved; cturthe'ṃśe — preserved; tryodśo'dhyāyḥ — above.; 13 — above.; o0o — above.

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

ityetdbhgvto mithyābhiśstikṣālnṃ yḥ smrti n tsy kdācidlpāpi mithyābhiśstirbhvti avyāhtākhilendriyścākhilpāpmokṣmvāpnoti ||162||

Śrīdhara

mithyābhiśstikṣālnāṃ mithyābhiśāpnivrttnopāybhūtṃ hricritṃ yḥ smrti | tsy mithyābhiśāpo n bhvti | kṛṣṇen svbhkte mṇiḥ sthāpit iti kṛṣṇābhiyogprihārāy pūrvṃ gupt ddhār, anenaivābhiprāyeṇ śtdhnvnā mṇau smrpymāṇe ydyntāvsyāmpi nānysy kthysi tārhyāsyāmītyuvāc | n tu srvjñkṛṣṇvñcnāy | yjñkvcdhārṇṃ c yādvbhyādev n tu kṛṣṇbhyāt | anythā mṇinā sh sbhāprveśo n ghṭte | ato'krūre mhābhāgvte mṇispṛhātiklpnmtimndmityupekṣṇīymiti bhāvḥ ॥162॥ iti śrīdhr-svāmi-kṛtāyām ātm-prkāśākhyāyāṃ svprkāśākhyāyāṃ vā śrī-viṣṇu-purāṇ-ṭīkāyāṃ cturthā'ṃśe tryodśo'dhyāyḥ smāptḥ ॥ ॥ iti śrī-viṣṇu-purāṇe cturthe'ṃśe tryodśo'dhyāyḥ ॥ ॥13॥ —o)0(o—

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

मिथ्याभिशस्तिक्षालनां मिथ्याभिशापनिवर्त्तनोपायभूतं हरिचरितं यः स्मरति । तस्य मिथ्याभिशापो न भवति । कृष्णेन स्वभक्ते मणिः स्थापित इति कृष्णाभियोगपरिहाराय पूर्वं गुप्त दधार, अनेनैवाभिप्रायेण शतधन्वना मणौ समर्प्यमाणे यद्यन्तावस्यामपि नान्यस्य कथयसि तार्ह्यास्यामीत्युवाच । न तु सर्वज्ञकृष्णवञ्चनाय । यज्ञकवचधारणं च यादवभयादेव न तु कृष्णभयात् । अन्यथा मणिना सह सभाप्रवेशो न घटते । अतोऽक्रूरे महाभागवते मणिस्पृहातिकल्पनमतिमन्दमित्युपेक्षणीयमिति भावः ॥१६२॥ इति श्रीधर-स्वामि-कृतायाम् आत्म-प्रकाशाख्यायां स्वप्रकाशाख्यायां वा श्री-विष्णु-पुराण-टीकायां चतुर्थाऽंशे त्रयोदशोऽध्यायः समाप्तः ॥ ॥ इति श्री-विष्णु-पुराणे चतुर्थेऽंशे त्रयोदशोऽध्यायः ॥ ॥१३॥ —ओ)०(ओ—

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 162).

Śrīdhara explains the preceding passage in relation to the chapter’s teaching; the exact Sanskrit note is preserved above.
